## Handoff channels and failover (Nov 2 2026; Augie: "We need more than a single point of failure on the chat handoff")
Tool: `python3 /workspace/aos-state/handoff/bus/handoff_bus.py` (send | check | watch | heartbeat | status). State and logs: /workspace/aos-state/handoff/bus/ (outbox/, bus.log, watch.log, last-watch.json). Tests: bus/tests/.
To hand work to DotBot: write the body to a file, then `handoff_bus.py send --id HO-<date>-<slug> --title "..." --body-file <f> [--tickets AUG-1,AUG-2]`. That posts channels 1 and 2 at once and writes the chat paste text to outbox/<ID>.chat.txt.

Forward: AugieOS/Tater -> DotBot
| # | Channel | Owner | Health check |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Linear issue labelled `dotbot-queue` (project Augie OS), listed in the Linear doc "DotBot inbox" | box handoff watcher (Tater app) | `send` reads back the issue id. Watcher re-renders the doc. DotBot acks with a comment "DotBot ack <ID>" |
| 2 | Git inbox: augsmog/augie-os `reports/dotbot-inbox/<ID>.md`, written via merge_gate (agent:tater) and pushed on demand with mirror_push.sh --only augie-os | box handoff watcher | `send` records merged/pushed. The watcher retries any unpushed copy every 10 min |
| 3 | ChatGPT dots thread (fast path and nudge only) | AugieOS browser (computerUse) | Manual. When bus/NUDGE-NEEDED.json exists, paste each listed outbox/<ID>.chat.txt |
| 4 | Fallback executor: box Codex lanes take items in bus/fallback-queue.json (DotBot handoffs with no ack in 6 h), plus the existing 6 h claim lapse in codex-lanes/dotbot-claims.json | box Codex lanes (common.md) | Lane runs are logged in codex-lanes/loop.log |
Receipt check (every 10 min): an ack is a non-Tater Linear comment starting "DotBot" on the queue issue, or on a listed ticket if it names the ID or says "DotBot worker". No ack after 45 min: retry failed channels, comment on the issue and write NUDGE-NEEDED.json. No ack after 6 h: the item lapses to fallback-queue.json.

Reverse: AugieOS down -> others take over
| # | Taker | Trigger / owner | Health check |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Box Codex failover run (bus/failover-prompt.md), at most one every 2 h while failover is active. It is skipped if inodes are at 76% or above, or Codex pace is not ok | box handoff watcher | Logs in bus/failover-runs/. Its vault writes use actor agent:failover |
| 2 | DotBot stands in (v2 standing instructions, section 4) | DotBot reads the Linear doc "AugieOS heartbeat" | The doc is rewritten about hourly. A tick older than 3 h means the box or the watcher is down, so DotBot takes over even if the box itself is dead |
| 3 | Mac Codex coordinator ("root") | Augie's Mac. It has had no active process since its Nov 3 00:48 UTC handoff (Nov 2 4:48 PM PT) | None automatic. Not counted as a live path |
AugieOS liveness is the newer of two signals: bus/augieos-heartbeat.json (`handoff_bus.py heartbeat --actor <routine>`) and the newest `agent:tater` vault commit (the bus's own dotbot-inbox commits are excluded). Stale means 6 or more daytime hours (07:00-23:00 PT) with no signal. On stale, the watcher writes bus/FAILOVER-ACTIVE.json and opens a Linear issue "ALERT: AugieOS silent since ..." (label failure), commenting on it every 6 h. It auto-closes when AugieOS is seen again. Alerts are Linear only, with no sends.
Notes: the git inbox is written to /workspace/augie-os, the merge-gated vault repo whose remote is augsmog/augie-os. /workspace/augie-os-vault is the Obsidian working copy and is not a git repo. The ack check is deliberately lenient: any DotBot comment on the queue issue counts. The failover Codex run uses the same flags as the box lanes, including the bypassed sandbox, because it needs git, network and Linear. Its limits come from failover-prompt.md and the hard rules.
Watcher self-health: handoff-watch.sh is restarted within 10 min by codex-lanes/lrd-runner-health.sh, which daily.sh runs. Its pid is in bus/watch.pid; stop it with `touch bus/STOP`. Backtest: the Nov 1-2 outage (last agent:tater commit Nov 1 12:21 AM PT) would have alerted by about 1 PM PT Nov 1 instead of going unnoticed for about 40 h.

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { chromium } from 'playwright';

const base = process.env.BASE_URL;
const out = process.env.OUT_DIR || 'e2e/journey-screens/pro-off-free';
if (!base) throw new Error('BASE_URL is required');
fs.mkdirSync(out, { recursive: true });
fs.rmSync(path.join(out, 'run-log.json'), { force: true });
const trace = [];
const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true, ...(process.env.PLAYWRIGHT_CHROMIUM_EXECUTABLE_PATH ? { executablePath: process.env.PLAYWRIGHT_CHROMIUM_EXECUTABLE_PATH } : {}) });
let page;
let startingRound = false;
try {
  const context = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width: 390, height: 844 }, serviceWorkers: 'block' });
  await context.routeWebSocket('**/*', socket => socket.close());
  await context.addInitScript(origin => {
    if (location.origin !== origin) return;
    if (!localStorage.getItem('pro-off-free-seeded')) {
      localStorage.setItem('snapscore-onboarding', JSON.stringify({ version: 0, state: { hasCompletedOnboarding: true, hasSeenDemo: true } }));
      localStorage.setItem('snapscore-scans', JSON.stringify({ version: 1, state: { tier: 'free', isPro: false, isTD: false, scanMonth: '', scansUsed: 0 } }));
      localStorage.setItem('pro-off-free-seeded', '1');
    }
  }, new URL(base).origin);
  page = await context.newPage();
  page.on('pageerror', error => {
    trace.push({ type: 'pageerror', message: error.message });
    if (startingRound && /Your plan does not include this hosting configuration/i.test(error.message)) {
      trace.push({ type: 'first-pro-gate', screen: 'skins-create', text: error.message });
    }
  });
  await page.goto(`${base}/`, { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
  trace.push({ type: 'boot', url: page.url(), onboarding: await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem('snapscore-onboarding')) });
  await page.getByTestId('home-new-contest').waitFor({ state: 'visible' });
  await page.getByTestId('home-new-contest').click();
  await page.getByPlaceholder('Your name', { exact: true }).fill('Flag Off Golfer');
  await page.getByPlaceholder('Player 2 name', { exact: true }).fill('Flag Off Partner');
  await page.getByPlaceholder('My Course', { exact: true }).fill('Flag Off Course');
  await page.getByTestId('contest-continue').click();
  const modes = page.getByTestId('contest-step-gamemodes');
  await modes.waitFor({ state: 'visible' });
  const skins = modes.getByTestId('game-mode-skins');
  await skins.waitFor({ state: 'visible' });
  await skins.click();
  assert.equal(await modes.getByTestId('skins-mode-net').count(), 1, 'Skins must be selectable as a former-Pro game');
  await page.getByTestId('contest-continue').click();
  await page.getByTestId('contest-step-round-settings').waitFor({ state: 'visible' });
  startingRound = true;
  await page.getByTestId('contest-start').click();
  let created = false;
  try { await page.getByTestId('contest-screen').waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 5000 }); created = true; } catch {}
  if (!created) {
    const gate = trace.find(item => item.type === 'first-pro-gate');
    if (gate) {
      await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'first-pro-gate-skins-create.png'), fullPage: true });
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'run-log.json'), JSON.stringify(trace, null, 2));
      console.error(`FIRST_PRO_GATE skins-create: ${gate.text}`);
      throw new Error('Flag-on journey stopped at the first Pro gate while creating the Skins round');
    }
    throw new Error('Round creation did not complete and no first Pro gate was observed');
  }
  startingRound = false;
  await page.evaluate(() => {
    const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('snapscore-contests'));
    const contest = saved.state.contests.at(-1);
    if (!contest || contest.groups.length !== 1 || contest.games.includes('skins') !== true) throw new Error(`Expected the newly created single-group Skins round: ${JSON.stringify({ active: saved.state.activeContestId, count: saved.state.contests.length, groups: contest?.groups?.length, games: contest?.games })}`);
    contest.groups[0].players.forEach((player, index) => { player.scores = Array.from({ length: contest.course.holes.length }, () => index === 0 ? 4 : 5); });
    localStorage.setItem('snapscore-contests', JSON.stringify(saved));
  });
  await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
  await page.getByTestId('contest-screen').waitFor({ state: 'visible' });
  const contest = page.getByTestId('contest-screen');
  await contest.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Settlement', exact: true }).click();
  const freeSettlement = page.getByTestId('settle-free');
  await Promise.race([
    freeSettlement.waitFor({ state: 'visible' }),
    page.getByTestId('settlement-scroll').waitFor({ state: 'visible' }),
  ]);
  const gated = await freeSettlement.count();
  if (gated) {
    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'first-pro-gate-settlement.png'), fullPage: true });
    const text = (await freeSettlement.innerText()).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    trace.push({ type: 'first-pro-gate', screen: 'settlement', text });
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'run-log.json'), JSON.stringify(trace, null, 2));
    console.error(`FIRST_PRO_GATE settlement: ${text}`);
    throw new Error('Flag-on journey stopped at the first Pro gate on Settlement');
  }
  await page.getByTestId('settlement-scroll').waitFor({ state: 'visible' });
  assert.notEqual(new URL(page.url()).pathname, '/paywall', 'Settlement must not redirect to the paywall route');
  const settlementText = (await page.getByTestId('settlement-scroll').innerText()).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  assert.match(settlementText, /\$\d+\.\d{2}/, 'Settle up must render an amount');
  assert.doesNotMatch(settlementText, /PaywallTeaser|Upgrade|Try Pro|See all plans|part of SnapScore Pro|free cloud scans this month/i);
  await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'settlement.png'), fullPage: true });
  trace.push({ type: 'settlement', text: settlementText });

  await page.goto(`${base}/`, { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
  await page.getByTestId('home-new-contest').waitFor({ state: 'visible' });
  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Profile', exact: true }).click();
  assert.notEqual(new URL(page.url()).pathname, '/paywall', 'Profile tab must not redirect to the paywall route');
  assert.equal(await page.getByTestId('profile-plan-badge').count(), 0, 'Profile must not render a Pro/Free plan badge while pro_enabled is off');
  const statistics = page.getByText('Statistics', { exact: true });
  await statistics.first().waitFor({ state: 'visible' });
  await statistics.first().click();
  await page.waitForURL(url => url.pathname === '/statistics');
  const statisticsHeading = page.getByText('Statistics', { exact: true }).last();
  await statisticsHeading.waitFor({ state: 'visible' });
  assert.equal(new URL(page.url()).pathname, '/statistics', 'Profile action must navigate to the Statistics screen');
  const statsText = (await page.locator('body').innerText()).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  assert.doesNotMatch(statsText, /Statistics with SnapScore Pro|See settlement, statistics, game variants, and unlimited cloud scans with Pro|Try Pro free for 7 days|Back to scores|free cloud scans this month|Upgrade to SnapScore Pro|Upgrade|Pro plan badge/i);
  assert.match(statsText, /Statistics/);
  await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'statistics.png'), fullPage: true });
  trace.push({ type: 'statistics', text: statsText.slice(0, 1200) });
  assert.deepEqual(trace.filter(x => x.type === 'pageerror'), [], 'page must not report runtime errors');
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'run-log.json'), JSON.stringify(trace, null, 2));
  console.log('PRO_OFF_FREE_PASS: skins selected, settlement amount rendered, Statistics opened without a Pro gate');
} catch (error) {
  try { await page?.screenshot({ path: path.join(out, 'failure.png'), fullPage: true }); } catch {}
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'run-log.json'), JSON.stringify([...trace, { type: 'failure', message: error.message }], null, 2));
  throw error;
} finally {
  await browser.close();
}

const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const test = require('node:test');

const scoresnap = path.resolve(__dirname, '../..');
const root = path.resolve(scoresnap, '..');
const flag = 'EXPO_PUBLIC_PRO_ENABLED';
const allowedFlagOnWorkflows = [
  '.github/workflows/tournament-e2e.yml',
  '.github/workflows/web-smoke.yml',
  '.github/workflows/xdevice-stack.yml',
].sort();

function filesBelow(directory) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(directory)) return [];
  return fs.readdirSync(directory, { withFileTypes: true }).flatMap((entry) => {
    const filename = path.join(directory, entry.name);
    return entry.isDirectory() ? filesBelow(filename) : [filename];
  });
}

function isTruthy(value) {
  if (typeof value === 'boolean') return value;
  if (typeof value === 'number') return value !== 0;
  if (typeof value !== 'string') return Boolean(value);
  return !['', 'false', '0', 'no', 'off'].includes(value.trim().toLowerCase());
}

function enabledWorkflowPaths(files) {
  return files
    .filter(({ name }) => /\.(yml|yaml)$/.test(name))
    .filter(({ source }) => new RegExp(`${flag}\\s*:\\s*["']?true\\b`, 'i').test(source))
    .map(({ name }) => name)
    .sort();
}

test('shipping EAS profiles cannot enable the Pro flag', () => {
  const eas = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(scoresnap, 'eas.json'), 'utf8'));
  for (const name of ['production', 'testflight', 'preview', 'simulator', 'simulator-offline']) {
    const profile = eas.build[name];
    assert.ok(profile, `missing EAS build profile: ${name}`);
    assert.equal(isTruthy(profile.env?.[flag]), false, `${name} must not set ${flag} to a truthy value`);
  }
});

test('sample env and TypeScript unset default keep Pro disabled', () => {
  const envExample = fs.readFileSync(path.join(scoresnap, '.env.example'), 'utf8');
  assert.match(envExample, new RegExp(`^${flag}=false$`, 'm'));

  const source = fs.readFileSync(path.join(scoresnap, 'src/config/pro-membership.ts'), 'utf8');
  assert.match(source, /String\(process\.env\.EXPO_PUBLIC_PRO_ENABLED\s*\?\?\s*["']false["']\)\.toLowerCase\(\)\s*===\s*["']true["']/);
});

test('only the explicit CI workflow allowlist may enable the flag', () => {
  const workflowRoot = path.join(root, '.github/workflows');
  const files = filesBelow(workflowRoot).map((filename) => ({
    name: path.relative(root, filename),
    source: fs.readFileSync(filename, 'utf8'),
  }));

  assert.deepEqual(enabledWorkflowPaths(files), allowedFlagOnWorkflows);
});

test('an out-of-allowlist flag-on workflow fixture fails the guard', () => {
  const fixture = [
    ...allowedFlagOnWorkflows.map((name) => ({ name, source: `${flag}: true` })),
    { name: '.github/workflows/unauthorized-fixture.yml', source: `env:\n  ${flag}: true\n` },
  ];
  assert.throws(() => assert.deepEqual(enabledWorkflowPaths(fixture), allowedFlagOnWorkflows));
});
